( NAME = N'DarouApp_log', FILENAME = N'C:\Program Files\Microsoft SQL Server\MSSQL16.MSSQLSERVER\MSSQL\DATA\DarouApp_log.ldf' , SIZE = 204800KB , MAXSIZE = 2048GB , FILEGROWTH = 65536KB )

/****** Object:  Table [dbo].[research_projects]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:31 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE TABLE [dbo].[research_projects](
	[project_id] [uniqueidentifier] NOT NULL,
	[project_code] [nvarchar](20) NOT NULL,
	[project_title] [nvarchar](200) NOT NULL,
	[project_type] [nvarchar](50) NULL,
	[project_category] [nvarchar](50) NULL,
	[project_description] [ntext] NULL,
	[objectives] [ntext] NULL,
	[expected_outcomes] [ntext] NULL,
	[methodology] [ntext] NULL,
	[risk_assessment] [ntext] NULL,
	[project_status] [nvarchar](20) NULL,
	[priority_level] [int] NULL,
	[current_phase] [nvarchar](100) NULL,
	[completion_percentage] [decimal](5, 2) NULL,
	[principal_investigator] [nvarchar](100) NULL,
	[project_manager] [nvarchar](100) NULL,
	[team_members] [ntext] NULL,
	[collaborators] [ntext] NULL,
	[start_date] [date] NULL,
	[planned_end_date] [date] NULL,
	[actual_end_date] [date] NULL,
	[total_budget] [decimal](15, 2) NULL,
	[spent_budget] [decimal](15, 2) NULL,
	[currency] [nvarchar](3) NULL,
	[resources_required] [ntext] NULL,
	[key_findings] [ntext] NULL,
	[publications] [ntext] NULL,
	[patents] [ntext] NULL,
	[awards] [ntext] NULL,

	[last_modified_date] [datetime] NULL,
 CONSTRAINT [PK_research_projects] PRIMARY KEY CLUSTERED 
(
	[project_id] ASC
)WITH (PAD_INDEX = OFF, STATISTICS_NORECOMPUTE = OFF, IGNORE_DUP_KEY = OFF, ALLOW_ROW_LOCKS = ON, ALLOW_PAGE_LOCKS = ON, OPTIMIZE_FOR_SEQUENTIAL_KEY = OFF) ON [PRIMARY]

/****** Object:  View [dbo].[vw_active_research_projects]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:31 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE VIEW [dbo].[vw_active_research_projects] AS
    SELECT 
        project_id,
        project_code,
        project_title,
        project_type,
        project_category,
        project_status,
        priority_level,
        completion_percentage,
        principal_investigator,
        project_manager,
        start_date,
        planned_end_date,
        total_budget,
        spent_budget,
        currency,
        created_date,
        CASE 
            WHEN total_budget IS NOT NULL AND total_budget > 0 
            THEN (spent_budget / total_budget) * 100 
            ELSE 0 
        END AS budget_utilization_percentage,
        CASE
            WHEN start_date IS NOT NULL AND planned_end_date IS NOT NULL
            THEN DATEDIFF(day, start_date, GETDATE()) * 100.0 / DATEDIFF(day, start_date, planned_end_date)
            ELSE 0
        END AS timeline_progress_percentage
    FROM research_projects 
    WHERE is_active = 1
GO
/****** Object:  View [dbo].[vw_project_summary_stats]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:31 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE VIEW [dbo].[vw_project_summary_stats] AS
    SELECT 
        project_status,
        project_type,
        project_category,
        COUNT(*) as project_count,
        AVG(completion_percentage) as avg_completion,
        SUM(total_budget) as total_budget_sum,
        SUM(spent_budget) as total_spent_sum,
        AVG(priority_level) as avg_priority
    FROM research_projects 
    WHERE is_active = 1
    GROUP BY project_status, project_type, project_category
GO

/****** Object:  View [dbo].[vw_account_balances]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:31 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE VIEW [dbo].[vw_account_balances] AS
SELECT 
    coa.account_id,
    coa.account_code,
    coa.account_name,
    coa.account_type,
    coa.normal_balance,
    COALESCE(SUM(jed.debit_amount), 0) as total_debit,
    COALESCE(SUM(jed.credit_amount), 0) as total_credit,
    CASE 
        WHEN coa.normal_balance = N'بدهکار' THEN COALESCE(SUM(jed.debit_amount), 0) - COALESCE(SUM(jed.credit_amount), 0)
        ELSE COALESCE(SUM(jed.credit_amount), 0) - COALESCE(SUM(jed.debit_amount), 0)
    END as balance
FROM chart_of_accounts coa
LEFT JOIN journal_entry_details jed ON coa.account_id = jed.account_id
LEFT JOIN journal_entries je ON jed.entry_id = je.entry_id AND je.status = N'تایید شده'
WHERE coa.is_active = 1
GROUP BY coa.account_id, coa.account_code, coa.account_name, coa.account_type, coa.normal_balance;

-- View for Trial Balance
GO
/****** Object:  View [dbo].[vw_trial_balance]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:31 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE VIEW [dbo].[vw_trial_balance] AS
SELECT 
    account_code,
    account_name,
    account_type,
    CASE WHEN balance >= 0 AND normal_balance = N'بدهکار' THEN balance
         WHEN balance < 0 AND normal_balance = N'بستانکار' THEN ABS(balance)
         ELSE 0 END as debit_balance,
    CASE WHEN balance >= 0 AND normal_balance = N'بستانکار' THEN balance
         WHEN balance < 0 AND normal_balance = N'بدهکار' THEN ABS(balance)
         ELSE 0 END as credit_balance
FROM vw_account_balances;

-- View for Income Statement
GO
/****** Object:  View [dbo].[vw_income_statement]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:31 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE VIEW [dbo].[vw_income_statement] AS
SELECT 
    account_type,
    account_code,
    account_name,
    balance as amount
FROM vw_account_balances
WHERE account_type IN (N'درآمد', N'هزینه') AND balance != 0;

-- View for Balance Sheet
GO

/****** Object:  View [dbo].[vw_OnlineUsers]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:31 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- Create a view for online users (active within last 30 minutes)
CREATE VIEW [dbo].[vw_OnlineUsers] AS
SELECT 
    u.Id,
    u.FirstName,
    u.LastName,
    u.Username,
    u.AvatarImg,
    us.Status,
    us.LastSeen
FROM 
    Users u
JOIN 
    UserStatus us ON u.Id = us.UserId
WHERE 
    us.LastSeen >= DATEADD(MINUTE, -30, GETUTCDATE());
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_CheckMaterialStock]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:32 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CheckMaterialStock]
    @MaterialId UNIQUEIDENTIFIER = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    SELECT 
        rm.material_id,
        rm.material_code,
        rm.material_name,
        rm.min_stock_level,
        rm.max_stock_level,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN mb.status = N'آزاد شده' THEN mb.current_quantity ELSE 0 END), 0) as available_stock,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN mb.status = N'قرنطینه' THEN mb.current_quantity ELSE 0 END), 0) as quarantine_stock,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN mb.status = N'رد شده' THEN mb.current_quantity ELSE 0 END), 0) as rejected_stock,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN mb.status = N'آزاد شده' THEN 1 END) as available_batches,
        u.unit_name,
        CASE 
            WHEN rm.min_stock_level IS NULL THEN 'حد آستانه تعریف نشده'
            WHEN COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN mb.status = N'آزاد شده' THEN mb.current_quantity ELSE 0 END), 0) <= rm.min_stock_level 
            THEN 'نیاز به سفارش خرید'
            WHEN COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN mb.status = N'آزاد شده' THEN mb.current_quantity ELSE 0 END), 0) <= (rm.min_stock_level * 1.5)
            THEN 'مانیتورینگ'
            ELSE 'وضعیت مطلوب'
        END as stock_status,
        MIN(CASE WHEN mb.status = N'آزاد شده' AND mb.expiry_date IS NOT NULL THEN mb.expiry_date END) as earliest_expiry
    FROM raw_materials rm
    LEFT JOIN material_batches mb ON rm.material_id = mb.material_id
    LEFT JOIN units u ON rm.unit_id = u.unit_id
    WHERE rm.is_active = 1
        AND (@MaterialId IS NULL OR rm.material_id = @MaterialId)
    GROUP BY rm.material_id, rm.material_code, rm.material_name, rm.min_stock_level, rm.max_stock_level, u.unit_name
    ORDER BY 
        CASE 
            WHEN rm.min_stock_level IS NOT NULL AND COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN mb.status = N'آزاد شده' THEN mb.current_quantity ELSE 0 END), 0) <= rm.min_stock_level 
            THEN 1 
            ELSE 2 
        END,
        rm.material_name;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_GetFEFOBatches]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:32 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_GetFEFOBatches]
    @MaterialId UNIQUEIDENTIFIER,
    @RequiredQuantity DECIMAL(10,3) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    SELECT 
        mb.batch_id,
        mb.batch_number,
        mb.current_quantity,
        mb.expiry_date,
        DATEDIFF(day, GETDATE(), ISNULL(mb.expiry_date, '9999-12-31')) as days_to_expiry,
        SUM(mb.current_quantity) OVER (
            ORDER BY ISNULL(mb.expiry_date, '9999-12-31') ASC, mb.batch_number ASC
            ROWS UNBOUNDED PRECEDING
        ) as cumulative_quantity,
        sl.location_name,
        rm.material_name,
        u.unit_name,
        CASE 
            WHEN @RequiredQuantity IS NOT NULL AND 
                 SUM(mb.current_quantity) OVER (
                     ORDER BY ISNULL(mb.expiry_date, '9999-12-31') ASC, mb.batch_number ASC
                     ROWS UNBOUNDED PRECEDING
                 ) <= @RequiredQuantity
            THEN mb.current_quantity
            WHEN @RequiredQuantity IS NOT NULL AND 
                 SUM(mb.current_quantity) OVER (
                     ORDER BY ISNULL(mb.expiry_date, '9999-12-31') ASC, mb.batch_number ASC
                     ROWS UNBOUNDED PRECEDING
                 ) - mb.current_quantity < @RequiredQuantity
            THEN @RequiredQuantity - (
                SUM(mb.current_quantity) OVER (
                    ORDER BY ISNULL(mb.expiry_date, '9999-12-31') ASC, mb.batch_number ASC
                    ROWS UNBOUNDED PRECEDING
                ) - mb.current_quantity
            )
            ELSE 0
        END as would_allocate
    FROM material_batches mb
    LEFT JOIN storage_locations sl ON mb.location_id = sl.location_id
    LEFT JOIN raw_materials rm ON mb.material_id = rm.material_id
    LEFT JOIN units u ON mb.unit_id = u.unit_id
    WHERE mb.material_id = @MaterialId
        AND mb.status = N'آزاد شده'
        AND mb.current_quantity > 0
    ORDER BY ISNULL(mb.expiry_date, '9999-12-31') ASC, mb.batch_number ASC;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_GetProjectsByStatus]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:32 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_GetProjectsByStatus]
        @ProjectStatus NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
        @IncludeInactive BIT = 0
    AS
    BEGIN
        SET NOCOUNT ON;
        
        SELECT 
            project_id,
            project_code,
            project_title,
            project_type,
            project_category,
            project_status,
            priority_level,
            completion_percentage,
            principal_investigator,
            project_manager,
            start_date,
            planned_end_date,
            total_budget,
            spent_budget,
            currency,
            created_date
        FROM research_projects
        WHERE 
            (@ProjectStatus IS NULL OR project_status = @ProjectStatus)
            AND (@IncludeInactive = 1 OR is_active = 1)
        ORDER BY 
            priority_level ASC,
            created_date DESC
    END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_UpdateProjectProgress]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:32 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_UpdateProjectProgress]
        @ProjectId UNIQUEIDENTIFIER,
        @CompletionPercentage DECIMAL(5,2),
        @CurrentPhase NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
        @SpentBudget DECIMAL(15,2) = NULL,
        @ModifiedBy UNIQUEIDENTIFIER = NULL
    AS
    BEGIN
        SET NOCOUNT ON;
        
        UPDATE research_projects 
        SET 
            completion_percentage = @CompletionPercentage,
            current_phase = ISNULL(@CurrentPhase, current_phase),
            spent_budget = ISNULL(@SpentBudget, spent_budget),
            last_modified_by = @ModifiedBy,
            last_modified_date = GETDATE()
        WHERE project_id = @ProjectId
        
        SELECT @@ROWCOUNT as AffectedRows
    END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_UpdateUserStatus]    Script Date: 8/13/2025 12:56:32 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- Create a stored procedure to update user status
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_UpdateUserStatus]
    @UserId UNIQUEIDENTIFIER,
    @Status NVARCHAR(20)
AS
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM UserStatus WHERE UserId = @UserId)
    BEGIN
        UPDATE UserStatus 
        SET Status = @Status, 
            LastSeen = GETUTCDATE() 
        WHERE UserId = @UserId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        INSERT INTO UserStatus (UserId, Status, LastSeen)
        VALUES (@UserId, @Status, GETUTCDATE());
    END
END;
GO
